- [ ] **Step 4: The rules**

`app/Support/Games/DrawAndGuessRules.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace App\Support\Games;

use App\Enums\GameKind;
use App\Enums\GameRoundOutcome;
use App\Models\GameGuess;
use App\Models\GamePlayer;
use App\Models\GameRoom;
use App\Models\GameRound;

class DrawAndGuessRules extends WordGuessRules
{
    public const int PointsPerFinder = 5;

    public const int WordChangesAllowed = 1;

    public function kind(): GameKind
    {
        return GameKind::DrawAndGuess;
    }

    public function prepare(GameRoom $room, GameRound $round, array $input): void
    {
        parent::prepare($room, $round, $input);

        $guessers = array_values(array_diff((array) ($input['guesser_player_ids'] ?? []), [$round->leader_player_id]));

        $round->guessers_total = $guessers === [] ? null : count($guessers);
    }

    public function presentActive(GameRound $round, GameRoom $room, ?GamePlayer $viewer): array
    {
        $finders = self::finders($round);
        $isFinder = $viewer !== null && in_array($viewer->id, array_column($finders, 'playerId'), true);
        $isLeader = $viewer !== null && $viewer->id === $round->leader_player_id;

        return [
            ...parent::presentActive($round, $room, $viewer),
            'finders' => $finders,
            'guessersTotal' => $round->guessers_total,
            'pointsPerFinder' => self::PointsPerFinder,
            ...($isFinder ? ['word' => (string) $round->word] : []),
            ...($isLeader ? ['wordChangesLeft' => max(0, self::WordChangesAllowed - $round->word_changes)] : []),
        ];
    }

    public function presentEnded(GameRound $round): array
    {
        return [...parent::presentEnded($round), 'finders' => self::finders($round)];
    }

    public function endedPayload(GameRound $round, GameRoom $room): array
    {
        return ['finders' => self::finders($round)];
    }

    public function expire(GameRoom $room, GameRound $round): ?GameRoundOutcome
    {
        return $round->hasFinders() ? GameRoundOutcome::Guessed : GameRoundOutcome::TimedOut;
    }

    public function expireTurn(GameRoom $room, GameRound $round): ?GameRoundOutcome
    {
        return $this->expire($room, $round);
    }

    public function outcomeOnNextRound(GameRound $round): ?GameRoundOutcome
    {
        return $round->hasFinders() ? GameRoundOutcome::Guessed : null;
    }

    /**
     * Each finder by the hints shown when they found, the drawer per finder;
     * a round without guessers scores as before (one winner).
     */
    public function points(GameRound $round, GameRoom $room): array
    {
        if (! $round->keepsFinding()) {
            return parent::points($round, $room);
        }

        $isGuessed = $round->outcome === GameRoundOutcome::Guessed;
        $finders = $isGuessed ? self::finders($round) : [];
        $rows = [];

        if ($round->leader_player_id !== null) {
            $rows[$round->leader_player_id] = ['points' => self::PointsPerFinder * count($finders), 'isWin' => false];
        }

        foreach ($round->guesses()->distinct()->pluck('player_id') as $playerId) {
            $rows[(string) $playerId] ??= ['points' => 0, 'isWin' => false];
        }

        foreach ($finders as $finder) {
            $rows[$finder['playerId']] = ['points' => $finder['points'], 'isWin' => true];
        }

        return $rows;
    }

    /**
     * Who found the word, in the order they found it; the guess text never
     * leaves the server.
     *
     * @return array<int, array{playerId: string, seconds: int, points: int}>
     */
    public static function finders(GameRound $round): array
    {
        return $round->guesses()
            ->where('is_correct', true)
            ->oldest()
            ->orderBy('id')
            ->get()
            ->map(fn (GameGuess $guess): array => self::finderEntry($round, $guess))
            ->values()
            ->all();
    }

    /**
     * @return array{playerId: string, seconds: int, points: int}
     */
    public static function finderEntry(GameRound $round, GameGuess $guess): array
    {
        return [
            'playerId' => $guess->player_id,
            'seconds' => max(0, (int) $round->started_at->diffInSeconds($guess->created_at)),
            'points' => self::guesserPoints($guess->hints ?? count($round->revealed_positions)),
        ];
    }

    protected function drawableOnly(): bool
    {
        return true;
    }

    protected function board(GameRound $round): array
    {
        return ['drawing' => $round->drawing];
    }
}
```

`WordGuessRules::points()` keeps the legacy path; its winner reads `count($round->revealed_positions)` as today (the round ended at the find, so it equals the hints of the guess). `expireTurn` and `outcomeOnNextRound` of `WordGuessRules` (Task 5) stay for Decoded.


(the wrong-guess broadcast after it is unchanged), and:

```php
    /**
     * Spec §6.15: the round goes on until every guesser has found; the first
     * finder is the round's winner, as the only finder was before.
     *
     * @return array{result: string, guessId: string, ended: ?array<string, mixed>, found: array{playerId: string, seconds: int, points: int}, word: string}
     */
    private function found(GameRoom $lockedRoom, GameRound $lockedRound, GamePlayer $player, GameGuess $guess): array
    {
        if ($lockedRound->winner_player_id === null) {
            $lockedRound->forceFill(['winner_player_id' => $player->id])->save();
        }

        $entry = DrawAndGuessRules::finderEntry($lockedRound, $guess);

        (new GameWordFound($lockedRoom, ['roundId' => $lockedRound->id, ...$entry]))->sendToOthers();

        $everyoneFound = $lockedRound->guesses()->where('is_correct', true)->count() >= (int) $lockedRound->guessers_total;

        return [
            'result' => GuessResult::Correct->value,
            'guessId' => $guess->id,
            'ended' => $everyoneFound ? $this->endGameRound->handle($lockedRoom, $lockedRound, GameRoundOutcome::Guessed) : null,
            'found' => $entry,
            'word' => (string) $lockedRound->word,
        ];
    }
```

- [ ] **Step 6: "New word"**

`app/Actions/Games/ChangeDrawWord.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace App\Actions\Games;

use App\Enums\GameKind;
use App\Events\Games\GameWordChanged;
use App\Models\GamePlayer;
use App\Models\GameRoom;
use App\Models\GameRound;
use App\Support\Games\DrawAndGuessRules;
use App\Support\Games\GameWord;
use App\Support\Games\WordGuessRules;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Symfony\Component\HttpKernel\Exception\ConflictHttpException;

class ChangeDrawWord
{
    public function __construct(private DrawGameWord $drawGameWord) {}

    /**
     * Spec §6.15: once per round, before anyone found; the drawing and the
     * letters shown go with the old word.
     *
     * @return array{roundId: string, word: string, mask: array<int, ?string>, maxHints: int, wordChangesLeft: int}
     */
    public function handle(GameRoom $room, GameRound $round, GamePlayer $player): array
    {
        return DB::transaction(function () use ($room, $round, $player): array {
            [$lockedRoom, $lockedRound] = LockGameRound::handle($room, $round);

            GameGuard::mutable($lockedRoom);
            GameGuard::roundGame($lockedRound, GameKind::DrawAndGuess);
            GameGuard::leader($lockedRound, $player);
            GameGuard::activeRound($lockedRoom, $lockedRound);

            if ($lockedRound->word_changes >= DrawAndGuessRules::WordChangesAllowed) {
                throw new ConflictHttpException(__('You have already changed the word.'));
            }

            if ($lockedRound->hasFinders()) {
                throw new ConflictHttpException(__('Someone has already found the word.'));
            }

            $word = $this->drawGameWord->handle($lockedRoom, true);

            $lockedRound->forceFill([
                'word' => $word,
                'revealed_positions' => [],
                'drawing' => [],
                'drawing_points' => 0,
                'word_changes' => $lockedRound->word_changes + 1,
            ])->save();

            $mask = GameWord::mask($word, []);
            $maxHints = WordGuessRules::maxHints($word);

            (new GameWordChanged($lockedRoom, ['roundId' => $lockedRound->id, 'mask' => $mask, 'maxHints' => $maxHints]))->sendToOthers();

            return [
                'roundId' => $lockedRound->id,
                'word' => $word,
                'mask' => $mask,
                'maxHints' => $maxHints,
                'wordChangesLeft' => max(0, DrawAndGuessRules::WordChangesAllowed - $lockedRound->word_changes),
            ];
        });
    }
}
```

```php
        return response()->json($changeDrawWord->handle($room, $round, $player));
    }
```

- Produces (types): `GameFinder = { playerId: string; seconds: number; points: number }`; `GameRound` gains `finders?: GameFinder[]`, `guessersTotal?: number | null`, `pointsPerFinder?: number`, `wordChangesLeft?: number`; `GameRoundEnded` and `GameRoundDetail` gain `finders?: GameFinder[]`; `GameWordFound = { roundId: string } & GameFinder`; `GameWordChanged = { roundId: string; mask: GameMask; maxHints: number }`; reducer actions `{type: 'word.found'; found: GameWordFound}` (appends to `finders` of the round it names, once per player) and `{type: 'word.changed'; change: GameWordChanged}` (sets `mask`, `maxHints`, empties `drawing` and `committedOpIds`); `startPayload(game, settings, leaderId, onlineOrder)` adds `guesser_player_ids: onlineOrder` for `draw` (the server drops the drawer). `use-game-channel.ts` lists `game.word.found` and `game.word.changed`; `use-game-room.ts` maps them to the two actions.

`lib/games/redo.ts`:

```ts
import type { DrawingOp } from './types';

/**
 * The drawer's redo list (spec §6.15): what Undo took, newest last. The
 * server knows nothing of it; a redo sends the op again as a new one.
 */
export type RedoStack = DrawingOp[];

export function pushUndone(stack: RedoStack, undone: DrawingOp | undefined): RedoStack {
    return undone === undefined ? stack : [...stack, undone];
}

export function popRedo(stack: RedoStack): { op: DrawingOp | null; rest: RedoStack } {
    if (stack.length === 0) {
        return { op: null, rest: stack };
    }

    return { op: stack[stack.length - 1], rest: stack.slice(0, -1) };
}

/** "Found by · 2 / 5": a player who joined after the start can still find. */
export function foundByTotal(finders: number, guessersTotal: number | null | undefined): number {
    return Math.max(finders, guessersTotal ?? 0);
}

/** "0:18". */
export function findTime(seconds: number): string {
    const minutes = Math.floor(seconds / 60);
    const rest = seconds % 60;

    return `${minutes}:${String(rest).padStart(2, '0')}`;
}
```

`lib/games/decoded.ts`:

```ts
import type { GameRound, GameRoundHistory } from './types';

export type DecodedPuzzle =
    | { state: 'done'; number: number; clue: string[]; word: string | null; finderName: string | null }
    | { state: 'current'; number: number }
    | { state: 'next'; number: number };

/**
 * Spec §6.16: the puzzles of the Decoded game in progress. The history is
 * newest first; the run is the unbroken chain of numbered Decoded rounds
 * just before the current one.
 */
export function decodedPuzzles(history: GameRoundHistory[], round: GameRound | null): DecodedPuzzle[] {
    if (round === null || round.game !== 'decoded' || round.number === null) {
        return [];
    }

    const done: DecodedPuzzle[] = [];
    let expected = round.number - 1;

    for (const entry of history) {
        if (expected < 1 || entry.game !== 'decoded' || entry.number !== expected) {
            break;
        }

        done.unshift({
            state: 'done',
            number: expected,
            clue: entry.clue ?? [],
            word: entry.word,
            finderName: entry.winnerName,
        });
        expected -= 1;
    }

    const coming: DecodedPuzzle[] = [];

    for (let number = round.number + 1; round.roundsTotal !== null && number <= round.roundsTotal; number++) {
        coming.push({ state: 'next', number });
    }

    return [...done, { state: 'current', number: round.number }, ...coming];
}
```

(read `GameRoundHistory` in `types.ts` for the exact names of `word` and `winnerName`, and the type name of a history entry; align.)

- [ ] **Step 2: Failing component test**, then build `decoded-puzzles.tsx`: heading "Puzzles" and "n / m" (or "n" when endless); a list (`<ol>`) of `.em-round` rows: done — the clue (`ClueRow` small, `aria-label` from the existing clue labelling), the word, "Found by :name" or "Not found"; current — `bg-skrum-primary-soft`, `aria-current="step"`, ":number · in progress"; next — dashed border (`border-dashed`), "Puzzle :number", nothing else. Mounted for Decoded in the left column of `room-panels.tsx`, above the settings card, for every viewer; on the phone the list is not shown and the round line of Task 16 ("Round 4 of 8") stands for it.

## Self-review (done while writing; kept for the reader)

**Spec coverage.** §6.1 games and outcome: Task 1 (enums), Tasks 11 to 14 (rules), Task 15 (front). §6.2 settings: Tasks 1, 3, 16. §6.3 themes: Task 2. §6.4 rounds and turns: Tasks 4, 5, 8, 14, 16. §6.5 auto hints: Tasks 6, 18. §6.6 hangman: Tasks 8, 17. §6.7 GIF: Tasks 9, 10, 19. §6.8 to §6.11 new games: Tasks 11 to 14, 20 to 23. §6.12 scores: Tasks 8, 10 to 14. §6.13 schema: Task 1. §6.14 secrecy: redaction tests of Tasks 9, 10, 11, 12, 13. §7 permissions: Tasks 3, 5, 8, 11, 13. §8 real time: Tasks 5, 8, 11 (`game.statements.changed`), 13 (`game.votes.counted`), 15. §6.15 Draw & Guess finders, new word, redo: Tasks 28, 30. §6.16 Decoded's puzzles: Tasks 28 (`clue`), 31. §9 screens: Tasks 15 to 23, 29 (§9.1 cards), 30 and 31 (§9.5). §10 migration of existing data: Task 1 (Upgrade test), Task 10 (one-vote rounds unchanged), Task 28 (Upgrade test, rounds without guessers). §11 routes: Tasks 5, 7, 8, 11, 13, 28. §12 testing: per task on PostgreSQL; races in Tasks 5, 8, 10, 12, 13, 28; captures in Task 25. §13 criteria: 1 → 3, 16; 2 → 1, 3; 3 → 2; 4 → 4, 16; 5 → 5; 6 → 5, 8; 7 → 8; 8 → 6, 18; 9 → 9, 10; 10 → 11 to 14, 20, 22 (prepared sets and a teller refused without one: Task 11; the draw of one among two or more and the single vote: Task 13); 11 → 9 to 13; 12 → 12, 13; 13 → 16, 17, 19, 25, 26; 14 → 15; 15 → existing `GameSnapshotTest` (run after each lane; Task 28 adds one query for Draw & Guess rounds only, constant); 16 → 24, 27; 17 → 29; 18 → 28 (`DrawFindersTest`), 30; 19 → 28 (`DrawNewWordTest`), 30 (redo); 20 → 28 (history `clue`), 31.

**Gaps found and closed while writing.** A room made by `EnsureIcebreakerRoom` takes turns from Task 3 on (decision 2), so two cases of `IcebreakerRoomTest` that start hangman without an order are edited in Task 5. The existing `GameRoomsController` requires `locale` for an icebreaker room: Task 3 makes it required without any setting (`required_without_all`), so the existing test keeps passing. The new games' payloads reuse the GIF game's names (`answers` pending, `voters`) so that the client's reducers serve them unchanged (Tasks 11, 12, 13). The GIF vote key is swapped in an order safe on MySQL (Task 1). Revision with the owner's answer 9 B: the Two truths set lives on the room (`game_statement_sets`), so the snapshot carries it (`truthSets`) even without a round, at one query and only for Two truths rooms (Task 11); the set is copied onto the round inside `StartGameRound`'s room lock (`prepare()` runs there, before the round is saved — read on `main`), which also makes "a set played twice" impossible; the Guess who? vote goes through the generic choice endpoint, so `game_choices.choice` and its validation take 36 characters (Tasks 1, 7); the drawn answer is a flag on `game_text_answers`, not a key on `game_rounds`, to avoid two tables pointing at each other (Task 1); Guess who? votes broadcast a count, not a player, since the author is the one player who never votes (Task 13). `gameSnapshotFor()` moves to `tests/Pest.php` (Task 3), since every lane uses it. Two tests list exactly four games and are edited (Task 1). Revision with the deviation answers: several finders apply only to a Draw & Guess round started with its guessers (`guessers_total`), so rounds in play at the upgrade, rounds started by an older client and Decoded keep ending at the first find and every existing word-guess test passes unchanged (Task 28); the guessers' number comes from the host's client like the turn order, since the server does not know who is online; a finder's points use the hints stored on their guess (`game_guesses.hints`), not the round's letters at the end, which keeps the scale of today's single winner; Redo needs no server change (the undone op is sent again as a new op); the card's players line never disables a card ("needs n more players" stays backlog); Decoded's list is built from the history the snapshot already carries (no new query), with `clue` added to Decoded entries.

**Placeholders.** Back-end tasks carry their tests and code. The word files of Task 2 are specified by index lists (the four files are parallel) and checked by counts; the prompts of Task 7 are written out in English and translated in Task 7, reviewed in Task 24. Task 28 carries its tests and code; Tasks 29 to 31 carry their pure logic in full (`catalogue.ts`, `redo.ts`, `decoded.ts`). Screen tasks 16 to 23 and 29 to 31 carry composition tables, behaviours, hooks and states, and the pure logic of Task 15 in full; the markup follows the mockups through the screen procedure of plan 18e.

**Type consistency.** `GameRules::expireTurn` is used by `ExpireGameRound` (time) and `EndGameTurn` (player or host) alike (Tasks 5, 8, 14). `AdvanceGameTurn::handle(…, bool $wraps = true): bool` is called by `HangmanRules` (wrapping) and `QuickQuestionRules` (not). `RevealsInStages::reveal` returns `?GameRoundOutcome` everywhere (null = next stage: GIF, Guess who's draw; an outcome: Two truths, Mood). `GuessWhoRules::expire` returns null after a draw, `TimedOut` under two answers and `Revealed` in the voting stage. `TakesChoices::choicesFor` returns strings; Two truths' `myChoice` is presented as an int, Mood's and Guess who's (a player id) as a string, and the TS type allows both. `PresentGifAnswers::revealed(…, bool $authorsHidden = false)` is called with the round's flag by `SprintGifRules::presentActive` and `RevealGifRound`, without it by `closedFrom`. The snapshot keeps `myVote` beside `myVotes`. `DrawAndGuessRules::finders()` feeds `presentActive`, `presentEnded`, `endedPayload` and `points` alike, and `finderEntry()` is the one shape of `game.word.found` and the guess response's `found`; TS `GameFinder` mirrors it. `DrawAndGuessRules::expire`, `expireTurn` and `outcomeOnNextRound` return `Guessed` with a finder, else the inherited outcome, and `PassGameRound` follows the same rule.

**Review Focus.** Each line has its test: a double turn end (Task 5 race), a stale turn job (Task 5), an upgraded room with an active GIF round (Task 1 Upgrade, Task 10), four votes at once (Task 10 race), leaks of a prepared set, the lie, a weather, an undrawn answer, an author or who voted (Tasks 11 to 13), a teller without a ready set or a set played twice (Task 11), two draws at once (Task 13 race), a pick out of turn and a double pick (Task 8 and its race), an empty or used-up themed pool (Task 2), the Draw & Guess word leaking or a double correct guess (Task 28 and its race), a round without guessers or upgraded (Task 28), a new word after a find or twice (Task 28).

**Known weak points of this draft.** Nothing was run. The PHPStan shapes of `BuildGameSnapshot` grow with every lane; a lane that breaks `types:check` fixes the shape in its own commit. `HangmanRules`' constructor changes in Task 5 (it gains `AdvanceGameTurn`); the anonymous test double of Task 7 follows it. The race of Task 10 may meet the per-player rate limit (burst 3) before the budget; the task says how to tell and what to do. The Guess who? draw is random: its tests read the stored `is_drawn` row and never a fixed answer. The points of the two games under answer 9 B (finder 5, teller or author 2 per player fooled) and the drawer's 5 per finder are rulings on the owner's "current point scale" (Owner decisions, rows 9′ and 11). Verification is PostgreSQL only; the MySQL unique swap of Task 1 and the new columns of Task 28 meet MySQL, MariaDB and SQLite only in the roadmap's final matrix (risk accepted by the owner). Auto hints already queued keep their times after a word change (Task 28): the new word may get its first letter early; accepted.
